Request: /api/components 19.06.2025 10:20:12

Executed DbCommand (38ms) [Parameters=[], CommandType='Text', CommandTimeout='30']
SELECT [s].[ManufacturerName], [s].[RuComponentKind], [s].[EnComponentKind], [s].[RuComponentType], [s].[EnComponentType], [s].[ComponentName]
FROM [ComponentTypes] AS [c]
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT [m0].[ManufacturerName], [c1].[RuComponentKind], [c1].[EnComponentKind], [c0].[RuComponentType], [c0].[EnComponentType], [m].[ComponentName]
    FROM [Microchips] AS [m]
    LEFT JOIN [ComponentTypes] AS [c0] ON [m].[Type_ID] = [c0].[ID]
    LEFT JOIN [Manufacturers] AS [m0] ON [m].[ManufacturerName_ID] = [m0].[ID]
    LEFT JOIN [ComponentKinds] AS [c1] ON [m].[Kind_ID] = [c1].[ID]
) AS [s] ON [c].[RuComponentType] = [s].[RuComponentType]
UNION ALL
SELECT [m1].[ManufacturerName], [c2].[RuComponentKind], [c2].[EnComponentKind], [c3].[RuComponentType], [c3].[EnComponentType], [t].[ComponentName]
FROM [Transistors] AS [t]
LEFT JOIN [Manufacturers] AS [m1] ON [t].[ManufacturerName_ID] = [m1].[ID]
LEFT JOIN [ComponentKinds] AS [c2] ON [t].[Kind_ID] = [c2].[ID]
LEFT JOIN [ComponentTypes] AS [c3] ON [t].[Type_ID] = [c3].[ID]
UNION ALL
SELECT [m2].[ManufacturerName], [c4].[RuComponentKind], [c4].[EnComponentKind], [c5].[RuComponentType], [c5].[EnComponentType], [r].[ComponentName]
FROM [Resistors] AS [r]
LEFT JOIN [Manufacturers] AS [m2] ON [r].[ManufacturerName_ID] = [m2].[ID]
LEFT JOIN [ComponentKinds] AS [c4] ON [r].[Kind_ID] = [c4].[ID]
LEFT JOIN [ComponentTypes] AS [c5] ON [r].[Type_ID] = [c5].[ID]
UNION ALL
SELECT [m3].[ManufacturerName], [c7].[RuComponentKind], [c7].[EnComponentKind], [c8].[RuComponentType], [c8].[EnComponentType], [c6].[ComponentName]
FROM [Capacitors] AS [c6]
LEFT JOIN [Manufacturers] AS [m3] ON [c6].[ManufacturerName_ID] = [m3].[ID]
LEFT JOIN [ComponentKinds] AS [c7] ON [c6].[Kind_ID] = [c7].[ID]
LEFT JOIN [ComponentTypes] AS [c8] ON [c6].[Type_ID] = [c8].[ID]
UNION ALL
SELECT [m4].[ManufacturerName], [c9].[RuComponentKind], [c9].[EnComponentKind], [c10].[RuComponentType], [c10].[EnComponentType], [d].[ComponentName]
FROM [Diods] AS [d]
LEFT JOIN [Manufacturers] AS [m4] ON [d].[ManufacturerName_ID] = [m4].[ID]
LEFT JOIN [ComponentKinds] AS [c9] ON [d].[Kind_ID] = [c9].[ID]
LEFT JOIN [ComponentTypes] AS [c10] ON [d].[Type_ID] = [c10].[ID]

) AS [s] ON [c].[RuComponentType] = [s].[RuComponentType]

SELECT [s].[ManufacturerName], [s].[RuComponentKind], [s].[EnComponentKind], [s].[RuComponentType], [s].[EnComponentType], [s].[ComponentName]
FROM [ComponentTypes] AS [c]
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT [m0].[ManufacturerName], [c1].[RuComponentKind], [c1].[EnComponentKind], [c0].[RuComponentType], [c0].[EnComponentType], [m].[ComponentName]
    FROM [Microchips] AS [m]
    LEFT JOIN [ComponentTypes] AS [c0] ON [m].[Type_ID] = [c0].[ID]
    LEFT JOIN [Manufacturers] AS [m0] ON [m].[ManufacturerName_ID] = [m0].[ID]
    LEFT JOIN [ComponentKinds] AS [c1] ON [m].[Kind_ID] = [c1].[ID]
) AS [s] ON [c].[RuComponentType] = [s].[RuComponentType]
UNION ALL

SELECT [s].[ManufacturerName], [s].[RuComponentKind], [s].[EnComponentKind], [s].[RuComponentType], [s].[EnComponentType], [s].[ComponentName]
FROM [ComponentTypes] AS [c]
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT [m0].[ManufacturerName], [c1].[RuComponentKind], [c1].[EnComponentKind], [c0].[RuComponentType], [c0].[EnComponentType], [m].[ComponentName]
    FROM [Microchips] AS [m]
    LEFT JOIN [ComponentTypes] AS [c0] ON [m].[Type_ID] = [c0].[ID]
    LEFT JOIN [Manufacturers] AS [m0] ON [m].[ManufacturerName_ID] = [m0].[ID]
    LEFT JOIN [ComponentKinds] AS [c1] ON [m].[Kind_ID] = [c1].[ID]
) AS [s] ON [c].[RuComponentType] = [s].[RuComponentType]
UNION ALL

LEFT JOIN [ComponentKinds] AS [c2] ON [t].[Kind_ID] = [c2].[ID]
LEFT JOIN [ComponentTypes] AS [c3] ON [t].[Type_ID] = [c3].[ID]
UNION ALL

SELECT [s].[ManufacturerName], [s].[RuComponentKind], [s].[EnComponentKind], [s].[RuComponentType], [s].[EnComponentType], [s].[ComponentName]
FROM [ComponentTypes] AS [c]
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT [m0].[ManufacturerName], [c1].[RuComponentKind], [c1].[EnComponentKind], [c0].[RuComponentType], [c0].[EnComponentType], [m].[ComponentName]
    FROM [Microchips] AS [m]
    LEFT JOIN [ComponentTypes] AS [c0] ON [m].[Type_ID] = [c0].[ID]
    LEFT JOIN [Manufacturers] AS [m0] ON [m].[ManufacturerName_ID] = [m0].[ID]
    LEFT JOIN [ComponentKinds] AS [c1] ON [m].[Kind_ID] = [c1].[ID]
) AS [s] ON [c].[RuComponentType] = [s].[RuComponentType]
UNION ALL

LEFT JOIN [ComponentTypes] AS [c1] ON [c].[Type_ID] = [c1].[ID]
LEFT JOIN [Manufacturers] AS [m] ON [c].[ManufacturerName_ID] = [m].[ID]
